     1	/**
     2	 * renderer/js/r3-0e-stores.js — R3.0E E2 · IndexedDB-backed experiment / outcome / timeline /
     3	 * follow-up-link stores (PRODUCTION).
     4	 *
     5	 * Uses the R3.0B storage-backend.js contract for atomic transactions:
     6	 *   backend.transact({ stores:[ns...], reads:[{store,key}...], compute:(readValues)=>({writes, result}) })
     7	 *
     8	 * Per SKYLINE D12/E1 ruling: R3.0E persistence lives in SEPARATE versioned stores and MUST
     9	 * NOT extend the frozen R3.0B portable case-record schema body.
    10	 *
    11	 * Stores:
    12	 *   - r3_0e_experiments       payload by experimentId
    13	 *   - r3_0e_experimentsIndex  summary index by experimentId
    14	 *   - r3_0e_outcomes          payload by outcomeId
    15	 *   - r3_0e_outcomesIndex     summary index by outcomeId
    16	 *   - r3_0e_timelines         payload by caseId (timeline-per-case append-only doc)
    17	 *   - r3_0e_followupLinks     payload by linkId
    18	 *   - r3_0e_followupLinksByCase  reverse index by parentCaseId (Array<linkId>)
    19	 *   - r3_0e_storeMetadata     migration / schema-version marker
    20	 *
    21	 * Persistence semantics:
    22	 *   - Every payload validated by its E1 contract BEFORE write (in compute()).
    23	 *   - Every payload re-validated on read; future-schemaVersion → fail-closed reject.
    24	 *   - Timeline events append-only; duplicate eventId rejected; out-of-order timestamp
    25	 *     rejected.
    26	 *   - Persisted records carry NO runtime authority (WeakSet identity is closure-private
    27	 *     and cannot survive reload). Rehydration consumers MUST re-validate via the E1
    28	 *     contracts before treating values as authoritative.
    29	 *
    30	 * UMD: Node require / Electron renderer global (R3_0E_Stores).
    31	 */

    57	    EXPERIMENTS_INDEX: 'r3_0e_experimentsIndex',
    58	    OUTCOMES: 'r3_0e_outcomes',

    67	    experiment: 1,
    68	    outcome: 1,
    69	    timeline: 1,

    73	  function _now(stamp) { return stamp || (typeof Date !== 'undefined' ? new Date().toISOString() : '1970-01-01T00:00:00.000Z'); }
    74	  function _err(code, extra) { var e = new Error(code); e.code = code; if (extra) Object.assign(e, extra); return e; }
    75	  function _deepFreeze(v) {
    76	    if (v === null || typeof v !== 'object') return v;
    77	    try { Object.freeze(v); } catch (e) { /* swallow */ }
    78	    var keys = Object.getOwnPropertyNames(v);
    79	    for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) _deepFreeze(v[keys[i]]);
    80	    return v;
    81	  }

    83	  // ========================================================================================
    84	  // Experiment store
    85	  // ========================================================================================
    86	  function createExperimentStore(backend) {
    87	    if (!backend || typeof backend.transact !== 'function') throw _err('R3_0E_STORE_BACKEND_INVALID');
    88	    return {
    89	      create: function (rec) {
    90	        var v = EXP.validateExperimentShape(rec);
    91	        if (v.valid !== true) return Promise.reject(_err('R3_0E_EXPERIMENT_INVALID', { reasonCodes: v.reasonCodes }));
    92	        if (rec.schemaVersion !== SCHEMA_VERSIONS.experiment) return Promise.reject(_err('R3_0E_EXPERIMENT_SCHEMA_MISMATCH'));
    93	        return backend.transact({
    94	          stores: [STORE_NAMES.EXPERIMENTS, STORE_NAMES.EXPERIMENTS_INDEX],
    95	          reads: [{ store: STORE_NAMES.EXPERIMENTS, key: rec.experimentId }],
    96	          compute: function (reads) {
    97	            if (reads[0] !== undefined && reads[0] !== null) throw _err('R3_0E_EXPERIMENT_ID_COLLISION');
    98	            return {
    99	              writes: [
   100	                { store: STORE_NAMES.EXPERIMENTS, key: rec.experimentId, value: rec },
   101	                { store: STORE_NAMES.EXPERIMENTS_INDEX, key: rec.experimentId, value: {
   102	                  experimentId: rec.experimentId, sourceCaseId: rec.sourceCaseId,
   103	                  status: rec.status, createdAt: rec.createdAt, updatedAt: rec.createdAt,
   104	                } },
   105	              ],
   106	              result: rec.experimentId,
   107	            };
   108	          },
   109	        });
   110	      },
   111	      update: function (rec) {
   112	        var v = EXP.validateExperimentShape(rec);
   113	        if (v.valid !== true) return Promise.reject(_err('R3_0E_EXPERIMENT_INVALID', { reasonCodes: v.reasonCodes }));
   114	        return backend.transact({
   115	          stores: [STORE_NAMES.EXPERIMENTS, STORE_NAMES.EXPERIMENTS_INDEX],
   116	          reads: [{ store: STORE_NAMES.EXPERIMENTS, key: rec.experimentId }],
   117	          compute: function (reads) {
   118	            var existing = reads[0];
   119	            if (existing === undefined || existing === null) throw _err('R3_0E_EXPERIMENT_MISSING');
   120	            if (existing.schemaVersion > SCHEMA_VERSIONS.experiment) throw _err('R3_0E_EXPERIMENT_FUTURE_SCHEMA');
   121	            if (existing.createdAt !== rec.createdAt) throw _err('R3_0E_EXPERIMENT_STALE_WRITE');
   122	            return {
   123	              writes: [
   124	                { store: STORE_NAMES.EXPERIMENTS, key: rec.experimentId, value: rec },
   125	                { store: STORE_NAMES.EXPERIMENTS_INDEX, key: rec.experimentId, value: {
   126	                  experimentId: rec.experimentId, sourceCaseId: rec.sourceCaseId,
   127	                  status: rec.status, createdAt: rec.createdAt, updatedAt: _now(),
   128	                } },
   129	              ],
   130	              result: rec.experimentId,
   131	            };
   132	          },
   133	        });
   134	      },
   135	      get: function (experimentId) {
   136	        return backend.transact({
   137	          stores: [STORE_NAMES.EXPERIMENTS],
   138	          reads: [{ store: STORE_NAMES.EXPERIMENTS, key: experimentId }],
   139	          compute: function (reads) {
   140	            var rec = reads[0];
   141	            if (rec === undefined || rec === null) return { writes: [], result: null };
   142	            if (rec.schemaVersion > SCHEMA_VERSIONS.experiment) throw _err('R3_0E_EXPERIMENT_FUTURE_SCHEMA');
   143	            var v = EXP.validateExperimentShape(rec);
   144	            if (v.valid !== true) throw _err('R3_0E_EXPERIMENT_CORRUPTED', { reasonCodes: v.reasonCodes });
   145	            return { writes: [], result: _deepFreeze(rec) };
   146	          },
   147	        });
   148	      },
   149	      list: function () {
   150	        return backend.list ? backend.list(STORE_NAMES.EXPERIMENTS_INDEX).then(function (rows) {
   151	          return rows.map(function (r) { return r.value; });
   152	        }) : Promise.resolve([]);
   153	      },
   154	      remove: function (experimentId) {
   155	        return backend.transact({
   156	          stores: [STORE_NAMES.EXPERIMENTS, STORE_NAMES.EXPERIMENTS_INDEX],
   157	          reads: [],
   158	          compute: function () {
   159	            return {
   160	              writes: [
   161	                { store: STORE_NAMES.EXPERIMENTS, key: experimentId, op: 'delete' },
   162	                { store: STORE_NAMES.EXPERIMENTS_INDEX, key: experimentId, op: 'delete' },
   163	              ],
   164	              result: experimentId,
   165	            };
   166	          },
   167	        });
   168	      },
   169	    };
   170	  }

   172	  // ========================================================================================
   173	  // Outcome store
   174	  // ========================================================================================
   175	  function createOutcomeStore(backend) {
   176	    if (!backend || typeof backend.transact !== 'function') throw _err('R3_0E_STORE_BACKEND_INVALID');
   177	    return {
   178	      create: function (rec) {
   179	        var v = OUT.validateOutcomeShape(rec);
   180	        if (v.valid !== true) return Promise.reject(_err('R3_0E_OUTCOME_INVALID', { reasonCodes: v.reasonCodes }));
   181	        return backend.transact({
   182	          stores: [STORE_NAMES.OUTCOMES, STORE_NAMES.OUTCOMES_INDEX],
   183	          reads: [{ store: STORE_NAMES.OUTCOMES, key: rec.outcomeId }],
   184	          compute: function (reads) {
   185	            if (reads[0] !== undefined && reads[0] !== null) throw _err('R3_0E_OUTCOME_ID_COLLISION');
   186	            return {
   187	              writes: [
   188	                { store: STORE_NAMES.OUTCOMES, key: rec.outcomeId, value: rec },
   189	                { store: STORE_NAMES.OUTCOMES_INDEX, key: rec.outcomeId, value: {
   190	                  outcomeId: rec.outcomeId, experimentId: rec.experimentId,
   191	                  class: rec['class'], createdAt: rec.createdAt,
   192	                } },
   193	              ],
   194	              result: rec.outcomeId,
   195	            };
   196	          },
   197	        });
   198	      },
   199	      get: function (outcomeId) {
   200	        return backend.transact({
   201	          stores: [STORE_NAMES.OUTCOMES],
   202	          reads: [{ store: STORE_NAMES.OUTCOMES, key: outcomeId }],
   203	          compute: function (reads) {
   204	            var rec = reads[0];
   205	            if (rec === undefined || rec === null) return { writes: [], result: null };
   206	            if (rec.schemaVersion > SCHEMA_VERSIONS.outcome) throw _err('R3_0E_OUTCOME_FUTURE_SCHEMA');
   207	            var v = OUT.validateOutcomeShape(rec);
   208	            if (v.valid !== true) throw _err('R3_0E_OUTCOME_CORRUPTED', { reasonCodes: v.reasonCodes });
   209	            return { writes: [], result: _deepFreeze(rec) };
   210	          },
   211	        });
   212	      },
   213	      listForExperiment: function (experimentId) {
   214	        if (!backend.list) return Promise.resolve([]);
   215	        return backend.list(STORE_NAMES.OUTCOMES_INDEX).then(function (rows) {
   216	          return rows.map(function (r) { return r.value; }).filter(function (r) { return r && r.experimentId === experimentId; });
   217	        });
   218	      },
   219	    };
   220	  }
   221	
   222	  // ========================================================================================
   223	  // Timeline store (append-only per-case)
   224	  // ========================================================================================
   225	  function createTimelineStore(backend) {
   226	    if (!backend || typeof backend.transact !== 'function') throw _err('R3_0E_STORE_BACKEND_INVALID');
   227	    return {
   228	      getTimeline: function (caseId) {
   229	        return backend.transact({
   230	          stores: [STORE_NAMES.TIMELINES],
   231	          reads: [{ store: STORE_NAMES.TIMELINES, key: caseId }],
   232	          compute: function (reads) {
   233	            var rec = reads[0];
   234	            if (rec === undefined || rec === null) {
   235	              return { writes: [], result: { schemaVersion: SCHEMA_VERSIONS.timeline, caseId: caseId, events: [] } };
   236	            }
   237	            if (rec.schemaVersion > SCHEMA_VERSIONS.timeline) throw _err('R3_0E_TIMELINE_FUTURE_SCHEMA');
   238	            var v = TL.validateCaseTimelineShape(rec);
   239	            if (v.valid !== true) throw _err('R3_0E_TIMELINE_CORRUPTED', { reasonCodes: v.reasonCodes });
   240	            return { writes: [], result: _deepFreeze(rec) };
   241	          },
   242	        });
   243	      },
   244	      appendEvent: function (caseId, event) {
   245	        return backend.transact({
   246	          stores: [STORE_NAMES.TIMELINES],
   247	          reads: [{ store: STORE_NAMES.TIMELINES, key: caseId }],
   248	          compute: function (reads) {
   249	            var existing = reads[0] || { schemaVersion: SCHEMA_VERSIONS.timeline, caseId: caseId, events: [] };
   250	            if (existing.schemaVersion > SCHEMA_VERSIONS.timeline) throw _err('R3_0E_TIMELINE_FUTURE_SCHEMA');
   251	            for (var i = 0; i < existing.events.length; i++) {
   252	              if (existing.events[i].eventId === event.eventId) throw _err('R3_0E_TIMELINE_DUPLICATE_EVENT');
   253	            }
   254	            if (existing.events.length > 0) {
   255	              var prevMs = Date.parse(existing.events[existing.events.length - 1].createdAt);
   256	              var newMs = Date.parse(event.createdAt);
   257	              if (isNaN(newMs) || newMs < prevMs) throw _err('R3_0E_TIMELINE_OUT_OF_ORDER');
   258	            }
   259	            var nextEvents = existing.events.concat([event]);
   260	            var next = { schemaVersion: SCHEMA_VERSIONS.timeline, caseId: caseId, events: nextEvents };
   261	            var v = TL.validateCaseTimelineShape(next);
   262	            if (v.valid !== true) throw _err('R3_0E_TIMELINE_INVALID', { reasonCodes: v.reasonCodes });
   263	            return { writes: [{ store: STORE_NAMES.TIMELINES, key: caseId, value: next }], result: event.eventId };
   264	          },
   265	        });
   266	      },
   267	    };
   268	  }
   269	
   270	  // ========================================================================================
   271	  // Follow-up link store
   272	  // ========================================================================================
   273	  function createFollowUpLinkStore(backend) {
   274	    if (!backend || typeof backend.transact !== 'function') throw _err('R3_0E_STORE_BACKEND_INVALID');
   275	
   276	    function _validateOne(rec) {
   277	      if (rec === undefined || rec === null) return null;
   278	      if (rec.schemaVersion > SCHEMA_VERSIONS.followUpLink) throw _err('R3_0E_LINK_FUTURE_SCHEMA');
   279	      var v = FU.validateFollowUpLinkShape(rec);
   280	      if (v.valid !== true) throw _err('R3_0E_LINK_CORRUPTED', { reasonCodes: v.reasonCodes });
   281	      return _deepFreeze(rec);
   282	    }
   283	
   284	    return {
   285	      create: function (link) {
   286	        var v = FU.validateFollowUpLinkShape(link);
   287	        if (v.valid !== true) return Promise.reject(_err('R3_0E_LINK_INVALID', { reasonCodes: v.reasonCodes }));
   288	        return backend.transact({
   289	          stores: [STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS, STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS_BY_CASE],
   290	          reads: [
   291	            { store: STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS, key: link.linkId },
   292	            { store: STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS_BY_CASE, key: link.parentCaseId },
   293	          ],
   294	          compute: function (reads) {
   295	            if (reads[0] !== undefined && reads[0] !== null) throw _err('R3_0E_LINK_ID_COLLISION');
   296	            var idx = reads[1];
   297	            var arr = (idx && idx.linkIds) ? idx.linkIds.slice() : [];
   298	            if (arr.indexOf(link.linkId) === -1) arr.push(link.linkId);
   299	            return {
   300	              writes: [
   301	                { store: STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS, key: link.linkId, value: link },
   302	                { store: STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS_BY_CASE, key: link.parentCaseId, value: { parentCaseId: link.parentCaseId, linkIds: arr } },
   303	              ],
   304	              result: link.linkId,
   305	            };
   306	          },
   307	        });
   308	      },
   309	      get: function (linkId) {
   310	        return backend.transact({
   311	          stores: [STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS],
   312	          reads: [{ store: STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS, key: linkId }],
   313	          compute: function (reads) {
   314	            return { writes: [], result: _validateOne(reads[0]) };
   315	          },
   316	        });
   317	      },
   318	      listForParent: function (parentCaseId) {
   319	        // 1. Read the reverse index.
   320	        return backend.transact({
   321	          stores: [STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS_BY_CASE],
   322	          reads: [{ store: STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS_BY_CASE, key: parentCaseId }],
   323	          compute: function (reads) {
   324	            var idx = reads[0];
   325	            // Codex E2-R2-01 closure: validate the reverse-index row itself.
   326	            if (idx === undefined || idx === null) return { writes: [], result: [] };
   327	            if (idx.parentCaseId !== parentCaseId) throw _err('R3_0E_LINK_CORRUPTED', { detail: 'reverse-index parentCaseId mismatch' });
   328	            if (!Array.isArray(idx.linkIds)) throw _err('R3_0E_LINK_CORRUPTED', { detail: 'reverse-index linkIds not array' });
   329	            return { writes: [], result: idx.linkIds.slice() };
   330	          },
   331	        }).then(function (linkIds) {
   332	          if (!linkIds.length) return [];
   333	          // 2. Read every link in one transact (atomic snapshot).
   334	          var readSpec = linkIds.map(function (id) { return { store: STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS, key: id }; });
   335	          return backend.transact({
   336	            stores: [STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS],
   337	            reads: readSpec,
   338	            compute: function (reads) {
   339	              // Codex E2-R1-02 closure: validate every fetched link before returning.
   340	              // Codex E2-R2-01 closure: ALSO verify each validated link's parentCaseId
   341	              // matches the caller-supplied parentCaseId. A poisoned reverse index that
   342	              // points at another parent's valid link is rejected as corruption rather
   343	              // than silently leaked across parent boundaries.
   344	              var out = [];
   345	              for (var i = 0; i < reads.length; i++) {
   346	                var validated = _validateOne(reads[i]);
   347	                if (validated === null) continue;

   376	  }

   function createFollowUpLinkStore(backend) {
     if (!backend || typeof backend.transact !== 'function') throw _err('R3_0E_STORE_BACKEND_INVALID');
 
     function _validateOne(rec) {
       if (rec === undefined || rec === null) return null;
       if (rec.schemaVersion > SCHEMA_VERSIONS.followUpLink) throw _err('R3_0E_LINK_FUTURE_SCHEMA');
       var v = FU.validateFollowUpLinkShape(rec);
       if (v.valid !== true) throw _err('R3_0E_LINK_CORRUPTED', { reasonCodes: v.reasonCodes });
       return _deepFreeze(rec);
     }
 
     return {
       create: function (link) {
         var v = FU.validateFollowUpLinkShape(link);
         if (v.valid !== true) return Promise.reject(_err('R3_0E_LINK_INVALID', { reasonCodes: v.reasonCodes }));
         return backend.transact({
           stores: [STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS, STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS_BY_CASE],
           reads: [
             { store: STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS, key: link.linkId },
             { store: STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS_BY_CASE, key: link.parentCaseId },
           ],
           compute: function (reads) {
             if (reads[0] !== undefined && reads[0] !== null) throw _err('R3_0E_LINK_ID_COLLISION');
             var idx = reads[1];
             var arr = (idx && idx.linkIds) ? idx.linkIds.slice() : [];
             if (arr.indexOf(link.linkId) === -1) arr.push(link.linkId);
             return {
               writes: [
                 { store: STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS, key: link.linkId, value: link },
                 { store: STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS_BY_CASE, key: link.parentCaseId, value: { parentCaseId: link.parentCaseId, linkIds: arr } },
               ],
               result: link.linkId,
             };
           },
         });
       },
       get: function (linkId) {
         return backend.transact({
           stores: [STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS],
           reads: [{ store: STORE_NAMES.FOLLOWUP_LINKS, key: linkId }],
           compute: function (reads) {
             return { writes: [], result: _validateOne(reads[0]) };
           },

       },
       listForParent: function (parentCaseId) {
         // 1. Read the reverse index.

           },

           if (!linkIds.length) return [];

             reads: readSpec,

+              // Codex E2-R2-01 closure: ALSO verify each validated link's parentCaseId

+              // points at another parent's valid link is rejected as corruption rather

    33	  // followUpCaseId / experimentId must all match the closed grammar.
    34	  var ID_GRAMMAR_RE = /^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,127}$/;
    35	  var ID_FORBIDDEN_RE = /(\.\.|[\/\\]|^\.)/;
    36	  function _idOk(s) {
    37	    if (typeof s !== 'string' || s.length === 0) return false;
    38	    if (ID_FORBIDDEN_RE.test(s)) return false;
    39	    if (!ID_GRAMMAR_RE.test(s)) return false;
    40	    return true;
    41	  }
    42	
    43	  function _isPlain(v) { if (v == null || typeof v !== 'object' || Array.isArray(v)) return false; try { var p = Object.getPrototypeOf(v); return p === Object.prototype || p === null; } catch (e) { return false; } }
    44	  function _hasOnlyAllowedKeys(o, allowed) { var keys; try { keys = Reflect.ownKeys(o); } catch (e) { return false; } for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) { var k = keys[i]; if (typeof k === 'symbol') return false; if (allowed.indexOf(k) === -1) return false; } return true; }
    45	  function _nonEmptyStr(v) { return typeof v === 'string' && v.length > 0; }
    46	
    47	  function validateFollowUpLinkShape(lIn) {
    48	    try {
    49	      if (!RC.isOriginalPlainObject(lIn)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.LINKAGE_INVALID, CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED]);
    50	      if (RC.hasHiddenOwnKey(lIn)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.LINKAGE_INVALID, CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY]);
    51	      var l = RC.toCleanCopy(lIn);
    52	      if (!_isPlain(l)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.LINKAGE_INVALID]);
    53	      if (!_hasOnlyAllowedKeys(l, LINK_KEYS)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.LINKAGE_INVALID, CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY]);
    54	
    55	      var reasons = [];
    56	      if (!Number.isInteger(l.schemaVersion)) reasons.push(CODES.LINKAGE_INVALID);
    57	      else if (l.schemaVersion > SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION) reasons.push(CODES.UNSUPPORTED_FUTURE_SCHEMA);
    58	      else if (l.schemaVersion < 1) reasons.push(CODES.LINKAGE_INVALID);
    59	      if (!_idOk(l.linkId)) reasons.push(CODES.LINKAGE_INVALID);
    60	      if (!_nonEmptyStr(l.parentCaseId)) reasons.push(CODES.LINKAGE_PARENT_MISSING);
    61	      else if (!_idOk(l.parentCaseId)) reasons.push(CODES.LINKAGE_INVALID);
    62	      if (!_idOk(l.followUpCaseId)) reasons.push(CODES.LINKAGE_INVALID);
    63	      if (l.parentCaseId === l.followUpCaseId) reasons.push(CODES.LINKAGE_INVALID);  // self-link
    64	      if (!_idOk(l.experimentId)) reasons.push(CODES.LINKAGE_INVALID);
    65	      if (PARENT_STATUS_ALLOWED.indexOf(l.parentStatus) === -1) reasons.push(CODES.LINKAGE_INVALID);
    66	      if (!_nonEmptyStr(l.createdAt)) reasons.push(CODES.LINKAGE_INVALID);
    67	
    68	      if (reasons.length) {
    69	        var seen = Object.create(null), out = [];
    70	        reasons.forEach(function (c) { if (!seen[c]) { seen[c] = true; out.push(c); } });
    71	        return RC.buildBlockedResult(out);
    72	      }
    73	      return Object.freeze({ valid: true });
    74	    } catch (e) { return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.LINKAGE_INVALID, CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION]); }
    75	  }
    76	
    77	  var api = { LINK_KEYS: LINK_KEYS, PARENT_STATUS_ALLOWED: PARENT_STATUS_ALLOWED, validateFollowUpLinkShape: validateFollowUpLinkShape };
    78	  Object.freeze(api);
    79	  if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) module.exports = api;
    80	  if (root) {
    81	    try { Object.defineProperty(root, 'R3_0E_FollowUpLinkContract', { value: api, writable: false, enumerable: false, configurable: false }); }
    82	    catch (e) { root.R3_0E_FollowUpLinkContract = api; }
    83	  }
    84	})(typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis : this);

 succeeded in 0ms:

    19	  else { fail += 1; console.log('  FAIL ' + msg + (detail !== undefined ? '  ' + JSON.stringify(detail) : '')); }

    68	(async function () {
    69	  var backend = createMemoryBackend();
    70	  var store = STORES.createExperimentStore(backend);
    71	  var rec = _validExperiment();
    72	  var id = await store.create(rec);
    73	  chk('A1: create returns id', id === rec.experimentId);
    74	  var got = await store.get(rec.experimentId);
    75	  chk('A2: get returns record', got && got.experimentId === rec.experimentId);
    76	  // Backend already structured-clones reads at the boundary, so callers can never mutate
    77	  // stored references. Verify the returned reference is distinct (mutation isolation) and
    78	  // schemaVersion-valid.
    79	  chk('A3: get returns mutation-isolated record (separate reference)',
    80	    got && got !== rec && got.schemaVersion === rec.schemaVersion);
    81	  // Duplicate id → collision
    82	  var ok = false;
    83	  try { await store.create(rec); } catch (e) { ok = e.code === 'R3_0E_EXPERIMENT_ID_COLLISION'; }
    84	  chk('A4: duplicate create → R3_0E_EXPERIMENT_ID_COLLISION', ok);
    85	  // Update OK
    86	  var updated = _validExperiment({ status: 'applied' });
    87	  var uid = await store.update(updated);
    88	  chk('A5: update returns id', uid === rec.experimentId);
    89	  var afterUpdate = await store.get(rec.experimentId);
    90	  chk('A6: updated record reflects new status', afterUpdate.status === 'applied');
    91	  // Stale generation: update with mismatched createdAt

    93	  var staleOk = false;

   104	  // Remove

/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba renderer/js/storage-backend.js | sed -n '1,190p'" in /Users/SKYLINE/Claude/projects/racing-setup-analyzer

     1	/**
     2	 * storage-backend.js — R3.0B: a namespaced async key/value backend with an IndexedDB-safe atomic transaction.
     3	 *
     4	 * Contract (all Promise-returning):
     5	 *   get(ns,key) · put(ns,key,val) · add(ns,key,val) [create-if-absent; rejects if present] · del(ns,key) ·
     6	 *   list(ns) → [{key,value}] · estimateBytes(ns?) ·
     7	 *   update(ns,key,fn) → atomic read-modify-write of one key (sugar over transact) ·
     8	 *   transact({stores:[ns...], reads:[{store,key}...], compute:(readValues)=>({writes:[{store,key,value}|{store,key,op:'delete'}], result})}) → Promise<result>
     9	 *
    10	 * transact is IndexedDB-safe: ALL reads are declared up front; `compute` is SYNCHRONOUS and PURE (no I/O, no await)
    11	 * and returns the writes + a result; the backend reads, then synchronously computes, then applies the writes, then
    12	 * commits — atomically. Two implementations:
    13	 *   - MemoryBackend  : tests / explicit SSR. A global mutex serializes transactions; values are structured-cloned
    14	 *                      at every boundary so callers can never mutate stored references.
    15	 *   - IndexedDBBackend: browser AND Electron renderer (IndexedDB exists in both). One readwrite transaction per
    16	 *                       transact(); aborts (no partial write) on error/quota, surfacing a typed StorageError.
    17	 * A renderer NEVER silently falls back to memory — IndexedDB unavailability surfaces STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE.
    18	 *
    19	 * UMD: Node require / Electron renderer global (StorageBackend).
    20	 */
    21	(function (root) {
    22	  'use strict';
    23	
    24	  function StorageError(code, message) { this.name = 'StorageError'; this.code = code; this.message = message || code; }
    25	  StorageError.prototype = Object.create(Error.prototype);
    26	  var CODES = { UNAVAILABLE: 'STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE', QUOTA: 'STORAGE_QUOTA_EXCEEDED', KEY_EXISTS: 'STORAGE_KEY_EXISTS', BAD_TXN: 'STORAGE_BAD_TRANSACTION' };
    27	
    28	  function _clone(v) {
    29	    if (v === undefined) return undefined;
    30	    if (typeof structuredClone === 'function') { try { return structuredClone(v); } catch (e) { /* fall through */ } }
    31	    return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(v));
    32	  }
    33	  function _byteLen(v) { try { return JSON.stringify(v).length; } catch (e) { return 0; } }
    34	
    35	  function _validateTxnSpec(spec) {
    36	    if (!spec || typeof spec !== 'object') throw new StorageError(CODES.BAD_TXN, 'spec required');
    37	    if (!Array.isArray(spec.stores) || !spec.stores.length) throw new StorageError(CODES.BAD_TXN, 'stores required');
    38	    if (spec.reads != null && !Array.isArray(spec.reads)) throw new StorageError(CODES.BAD_TXN, 'reads must be an array');
    39	    if (typeof spec.compute !== 'function') throw new StorageError(CODES.BAD_TXN, 'compute must be a function');
    40	  }
    41	  // apply a compute() result shape; returns the writes array (validated) — pure
    42	  function _normalizeWrites(out, stores) {
    43	    if (!out || typeof out !== 'object') throw new StorageError(CODES.BAD_TXN, 'compute must return an object');
    44	    var writes = out.writes || [];
    45	    if (!Array.isArray(writes)) throw new StorageError(CODES.BAD_TXN, 'writes must be an array');
    46	    writes.forEach(function (w) {
    47	      if (!w || stores.indexOf(w.store) === -1 || typeof w.key !== 'string') throw new StorageError(CODES.BAD_TXN, 'invalid write');
    48	    });
    49	    return writes;
    50	  }
    51	
    52	  // ── MemoryBackend ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    53	  function MemoryBackend() {
    54	    var data = {}; // ns -> { key -> value }
    55	    var chain = Promise.resolve(); // global mutex
    56	    function nsOf(ns) { if (!data[ns]) data[ns] = {}; return data[ns]; }
    57	    function run(fn) { var p = chain.then(function () { return fn(); }); chain = p.then(function () { }, function () { }); return p; }
    58	    return {
    59	      _kind: 'memory',
    60	      get: function (ns, key) { return run(function () { return _clone(nsOf(ns)[key]); }); },
    61	      put: function (ns, key, val) { return run(function () { nsOf(ns)[key] = _clone(val); }); },
    62	      add: function (ns, key, val) { return run(function () { var s = nsOf(ns); if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(s, key)) throw new StorageError(CODES.KEY_EXISTS, key); s[key] = _clone(val); }); },
    63	      del: function (ns, key) { return run(function () { delete nsOf(ns)[key]; }); },
    64	      list: function (ns) { return run(function () { var s = nsOf(ns); return Object.keys(s).map(function (k) { return { key: k, value: _clone(s[k]) }; }); }); },
    65	      estimateBytes: function (ns) { return run(function () { if (ns) { var s = nsOf(ns); return Object.keys(s).reduce(function (a, k) { return a + _byteLen(s[k]); }, 0); } return Object.keys(data).reduce(function (a, n) { var ss = data[n]; return a + Object.keys(ss).reduce(function (b, k) { return b + _byteLen(ss[k]); }, 0); }, 0); }); },
    66	      transact: function (spec) {
    67	        return run(function () {
    68	          _validateTxnSpec(spec);
    69	          var readValues = (spec.reads || []).map(function (r) { return _clone(nsOf(r.store)[r.key]); });
    70	          var out = spec.compute(readValues); // SYNCHRONOUS pure
    71	          var writes = _normalizeWrites(out, spec.stores);
    72	          // apply atomically (memory is single-threaded under the mutex)
    73	          writes.forEach(function (w) { if (w.op === 'delete') delete nsOf(w.store)[w.key]; else nsOf(w.store)[w.key] = _clone(w.value); });
    74	          return _clone(out.result);
    75	        });
    76	      },
    77	      update: function (ns, key, fn) { return this.transact({ stores: [ns], reads: [{ store: ns, key: key }], compute: function (rv) { var next = fn(rv[0]); return { writes: [{ store: ns, key: key, value: next }], result: next }; } }); },
    78	    };
    79	  }
    80	
    81	  // ── IndexedDBBackend (browser + Electron renderer) ───────────────────────────
    82	  function IndexedDBBackend(opts) {
    83	    opts = opts || {};
    84	    var idb = (typeof indexedDB !== 'undefined') ? indexedDB : (root && root.indexedDB);
    85	    if (!idb) throw new StorageError(CODES.UNAVAILABLE, 'indexedDB not available');
    86	    var dbName = opts.dbName || 'racing_analyzer_store';
    87	    var stores = opts.stores || ['cases', 'caseIndex', 'sessions', 'sessionIndex', 'meta'];
    88	    var version = opts.version || 1;
    89	    var _db = null;
    90	    function open() {
    91	      if (_db) return Promise.resolve(_db);
    92	      return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
    93	        var req;
    94	        try { req = idb.open(dbName, version); } catch (e) { reject(new StorageError(CODES.UNAVAILABLE, String(e && e.message || e))); return; }
    95	        req.onupgradeneeded = function () { var db = req.result; stores.forEach(function (s) { if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(s)) db.createObjectStore(s); }); };
    96	        req.onsuccess = function () { _db = req.result; resolve(_db); };
    97	        req.onerror = function () { reject(new StorageError(CODES.UNAVAILABLE, String(req.error && req.error.message || 'open failed'))); };
    98	      });
    99	    }
   100	    function reqP(request, onerr) { return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) { request.onsuccess = function () { resolve(request.result); }; request.onerror = function () { reject(onerr ? onerr(request.error) : request.error); }; }); }
   101	    function single(storeNames, mode, fn) {
   102	      return open().then(function (db) {
   103	        return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
   104	          var tx, result, aborted = false;
   105	          try { tx = db.transaction(storeNames, mode); } catch (e) { reject(new StorageError(CODES.BAD_TXN, String(e && e.message || e))); return; }
   106	          tx.oncomplete = function () { resolve(result); };
   107	          tx.onabort = function () { if (!aborted) reject(_quotaOr(tx.error)); };
   108	          tx.onerror = function () { /* abort handler resolves */ };
   109	          try { fn(tx, function (r) { result = r; }, function (e) { aborted = true; try { tx.abort(); } catch (_) {} reject(e); }); }
   110	          catch (e) { aborted = true; try { tx.abort(); } catch (_) {} reject(e instanceof StorageError ? e : new StorageError(CODES.BAD_TXN, String(e && e.message || e))); }
   111	        });
   112	      });
   113	    }
   114	    function _quotaOr(err) { var n = err && err.name; if (n === 'QuotaExceededError' || n === 'NS_ERROR_DOM_QUOTA_REACHED') return new StorageError(CODES.QUOTA, 'quota exceeded'); return new StorageError(CODES.BAD_TXN, String((err && err.message) || 'transaction aborted')); }
   115	    return {
   116	      _kind: 'indexeddb',
   117	      get: function (ns, key) { return single([ns], 'readonly', function (tx, done) { var r = tx.objectStore(ns).get(key); r.onsuccess = function () { done(r.result); }; }); },
   118	      put: function (ns, key, val) { return single([ns], 'readwrite', function (tx) { tx.objectStore(ns).put(val, key); }); },
   119	      add: function (ns, key, val) { return single([ns], 'readwrite', function (tx, done, fail) { var r = tx.objectStore(ns).add(val, key); r.onerror = function () { fail(new StorageError(CODES.KEY_EXISTS, key)); }; }); },
   120	      del: function (ns, key) { return single([ns], 'readwrite', function (tx) { tx.objectStore(ns).delete(key); }); },
   121	      list: function (ns) { return single([ns], 'readonly', function (tx, done) { var out = []; var cur = tx.objectStore(ns).openCursor(); cur.onsuccess = function () { var c = cur.result; if (c) { out.push({ key: c.key, value: c.value }); c.continue(); } else done(out); }; }); },
   122	      estimateBytes: function (ns) {
   123	        if (navigator && navigator.storage && navigator.storage.estimate && !ns) return navigator.storage.estimate().then(function (e) { return e.usage || 0; });
   124	        var names = ns ? [ns] : stores; var total = 0; var self = this;
   125	        return names.reduce(function (p, n) { return p.then(function () { return self.list(n).then(function (rows) { rows.forEach(function (r) { total += _byteLen(r.value); }); }); }); }, Promise.resolve()).then(function () { return total; });
   126	      },
   127	      // IndexedDB-safe: declared reads → synchronous compute → writes, all in ONE readwrite transaction.
   128	      transact: function (spec) {
   129	        _validateTxnSpec(spec);
   130	        return single(spec.stores, 'readwrite', function (tx, done, fail) {
   131	          var reads = spec.reads || [];
   132	          var values = new Array(reads.length); var pending = reads.length;
   133	          if (!pending) { applyCompute(); return; }
   134	          reads.forEach(function (r, i) { var rq = tx.objectStore(r.store).get(r.key); rq.onsuccess = function () { values[i] = rq.result; if (--pending === 0) applyCompute(); }; });
   135	          function applyCompute() {
   136	            // SYNCHRONOUS from the final read's success callback — no await/Promise hop (keeps the txn alive)
   137	            var out;
   138	            try { out = spec.compute(values.map(_clone)); var writes = _normalizeWrites(out, spec.stores); writes.forEach(function (w) { var os = tx.objectStore(w.store); if (w.op === 'delete') os.delete(w.key); else os.put(w.value, w.key); }); done(out.result); }
   139	            catch (e) { fail(e instanceof StorageError ? e : new StorageError(CODES.BAD_TXN, String(e && e.message || e))); }
   140	          }
   141	        });
   142	      },
   143	      update: function (ns, key, fn) { return this.transact({ stores: [ns], reads: [{ store: ns, key: key }], compute: function (rv) { var next = fn(rv[0]); return { writes: [{ store: ns, key: key, value: next }], result: next }; } }); },
   144	    };
   145	  }
   146	
   147	  var api = { MemoryBackend: MemoryBackend, IndexedDBBackend: IndexedDBBackend, StorageError: StorageError, CODES: CODES };
   148	  if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) module.exports = api;
   149	  if (root) root.StorageBackend = api;
   150	})(typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis : this);
